Event/run identifiers must be opaque and validated. Metadata fields such as model, task and status are constrained against trusted registries, not free-form strings that can carry a prompt. Detailed permitted evidence lives in the authorized test/knowledge environment; do not copy private input/output into Operator merely because the evidence schema contains a reference field.

27.2 Structural checks to implement and run

The contract linter must check all of the following before a plan or release report claims complete coverage:

  1. All 55 established requirement IDs, 112 unique catalog use-case IDs and 66 unique root task IDs exist. Preserve the previous IDs and mappings; new requirements need an explicit owner-reviewed diff.
  2. Every owner commitment has requirement, task and proof anchors. Every individual use case has an implementation and a matching actual test definition. No range is treated as one passing test.
  3. Every referenced requirement, task, suffix slice, source and flow exists. Common implementation dependencies and scope-specific integration gates resolve to an acyclic graph with valid outputs. Distinguish an adapter contract from its later end-to-end acceptance to avoid circular prerequisites.
  4. Every applicable advertised requirement has evidence at its required level. IMPLEMENTED, MOCKED, NOT_TESTED, BLOCKED, skipped and unsupported are not VERIFIED. Invalid/missing artifact or deployment identity rejects a release-level receipt.
  5. Feature flags, failed tests, disabled integrations and unresolved external prerequisites cannot disappear from the final denominator. A narrower release requires explicit owner scope approval and truthful release notes.
  6. Final Windows acceptance references the signed artifact hash after all required signing/timestamping. Published/downloaded hash must match. A changed byte, helper, manifest or relevant backend/policy invalidates affected old evidence.
  7. All performance/quality claims name measurement boundary, sample size, hardware/network, dataset/scoring, errors/abstentions and proposed budget or qualified result. A source-regex test or simulated view cannot satisfy a native or live-service claim.
  8. Sources and external prerequisites are not automatically refreshed by a passing code test. Track review date, applicable version, assurance owner and conditions that require requalification.
  9. The original 20 historical findings have current VERIFIED, NOT_REPRODUCED_WITH_EVIDENCE, BLOCKED or owner-approved disposition. Neither an old report nor a branch title proves present status.
  10. The short Codex launch text references the same full scope as the registry. The document's former UC-076 cutoff must not return through a stale template.

These are checks on traceability and receipts, not an algorithm that can certify legal compliance or semantic truth. A real reviewer still verifies whether the cited evidence supports the claim. Codex must never self-approve a permission expansion, legal basis or publisher identity.

27.3 Lifecycle states and stale evidence

Use separate fields for implementation state, deployment state, verification result and applicability. A single status string such as “done” hides too much. A source change can preserve a still-valid independent component result only through an explicit impact review; final artifact acceptance always binds the actual tested artifact. A newer provider alias, rubric, policy, source ACL or DB migration may invalidate relevant evidence even if desktop code did not change.

Track a parent task as PARTIAL when some required slices pass and others do not. Keep a passed .WIN result usable without waiting on .MAC public credentials. Keep a failed privacy/authorization gate blocking every dependent content-processing claim. A mocked .CONTRACT slice permits independent development but never unlocks real customer content or public promotion.

27.4 The release decision algorithm

For each proposed platform/profile release, resolve its owner-approved advertised scope. Expand the required shared controls, exact capabilities, use cases and service dependencies. Require actual compatible evidence for each applicable row and a reviewed disposition for every excluded/blocked row. Reject unresolved critical security/privacy failures, missing mandatory native/quality/operation evidence, inaccurate labels or a changed final artifact.

For Windows, require legitimate signer/timestamp and verified post-upload bytes. For native Mac public distribution, require its actual Apple route prerequisites in addition to native functional evidence. For Teams/live media, require its actual permission/status/hosting/privacy prerequisites; a personal tab or imported transcript does not satisfy them. Require meaningful recovery and the actual deployed backend, not only source configuration.

Publish only the frozen approved artifact. Mark the whole Métis 2.0 contract complete only when all promised required scopes are satisfied or the owner has explicitly revised the contract; a scoped release and full-contract completion are different decisions.

27.5 Final human-readable report

Deliver one concise summary containing: changes and existing paths reused; source and deployed identities; the scope actually shipped; all 112 case statuses by relevant platform/profile; the 55 requirement dispositions; golden-flow receipts; transcript and critical-error scores; measured latency/size/resource improvement; actual Jev/Laya and Intelligence results; canonical/Dust/skill correctness; Teams/privacy/rights evidence; signature/hash/download evidence; recovery results; and the smallest unresolved blockers with their owners and next operations.

Include a limited residual-risk register instead of an unexplained readiness score. Specifically name inaccessible source inputs, supplier/tenant approvals, unsupported hardware/platform conditions, uncertainty in transcript/knowledge quality, retention limits outside controlled sinks and missing final native evidence. Explain which features still work under failure without weakening policy. The next Codex session must be able to resume from CURRENT.md and the actual working tree without guessing what was done.

27.6 What was validated while preparing this revision

The preparation script checks preservation and uniqueness of the 55 requirement IDs, 112 catalog rows and 66 task definitions; coverage of all requirements by owner commitments; exhaustive, non-overlapping use-case ownership ranges; valid task/requirement/flow references; the acyclic common-prerequisite graph; retention of all 20 historical findings; and correction of the stale launch instruction and signing order. The PDF is rendered and inspected separately for layout/text completeness.

This is document validation only. No deployed task graph, production database, application test suite, microphone, Jev/Laya service, Teams meeting, Dust write, native installer or signing operation has been executed by this revision's preparation. Codex must produce and validate the real evidence, including the detailed scope-specific graph and test definitions, before closing product gates.

Final acceptance principle: a verified user, under the effective policy, asks for a supported action; the correct backend helps only where needed; trusted code performs and verifies the right result; the transcript remains faithful; cloud content persistence is prevented under the qualified route; local models remain explicit and device-qualified; consumption is attributable; the application stays responsive; installation is proportionate; and the actual released binary can be trusted. Shared knowledge remains source-backed and permissioned; Dust changes are verified canonical updates; skills are centrally governed; meeting participation is visible, supported and authorized. Anything less remains a visible engineering task, not a marketing claim.

28. Complete meeting experience and competitive qualification

Competition is an outcome target, not an official certification or affiliation. The requested comparison set is Cluely, Plaud, Demodesk and Granola. Their publicly described workflows informed these qualifications; no hands-on head-to-head result is claimed. See R80–R85. Do not rebuild another product or add every competitor feature. Preserve Métis’s distinctive combination: visible trusted assistance, verified actions, source-backed shared knowledge and centrally governed skills.

The twelve EXP contracts below are mandatory refinements of existing capabilities. Keep all baseline M2/UC/TASK IDs; record separate untested EXP receipts and source findings. Each EXP must have individual actual test definitions and applicable platform/service slices before closing. A group-level screenshot is not twelve passes.

SRC-01 — Do not mistake a source export for a complete checkout

P0 · INPUT_LIMITATION · Owners: TASK-001, TASK-002, TASK-003, TASK-066.

Source observation: The export contains 1,521 FILE sections and package version 1.9.5. Five of the 19 file-backed nodes in the supplied map have no source section: renderer App.tsx, main index.ts, renderer lib/listen.ts, main/transcripts.ts and main/brain/ingest.ts. Imports and tests mention them; that does not recover their contents. The filename suffix is not an established Git commit.

Required change: Acquire the authorized real checkout and its commit/dirty state. Compare source hashes before using anchors. Preserve the export as historical evidence; never generate stub replacements or run an incomplete reconstructed app as the baseline.

Exit evidence: Exact checkout/branch identity and a missing-versus-present source manifest; baseline checks on the complete tree, with every skipped suite explained.

Anchors: package.json. [R80]

SRC-02 — Separate entitlement authority from telemetry and legacy licensing

P0 · ARCHITECTURE_GAP · Owners: TASK-002, TASK-005, TASK-006, TASK-014, TASK-034, TASK-045, TASK-062, TASK-063.

Source observation: The map separates a license service but omits the consumer edges. Operator has issued-license/seat records, while the legacy service has a serialized JSON store and a separately configured offline lease key. The legacy module describes compiled-off UI gates; the corresponding current renderer files are absent, so actual gate enablement is not established.

Required change: Choose one issuer/entitlement authority for each active distribution profile. Entra authenticates identity; a signed license or lease grants only its exact entitlement; telemetry supplies neither identity proof nor permission. Reconcile legacy and Operator IDs without switching on conflicting gates. Production rejects development trust roots. Preserve recovery/export access when paid inference is unavailable.

Exit evidence: Activation, renewal, revoke, expired/clock-skewed lease, duplicated device, reinstall, entitlement loss, two-user isolation and cross-process seat-cap races on the actual chosen store. JSON mutex cannot be represented as a distributed lock.

Anchors: operator/src/store.ts; license-server/lib/store.mjs; src/main/license.ts; src/main/license-lease-key.ts. [R80]

SRC-03 — Apply the Jev decision instead of discarding it

P0 · SOURCE_DEFECT · Owners: TASK-005, TASK-031, TASK-032, TASK-033, TASK-040, TASK-056.

SRC-04 — Execution result and exception handling must control completion

P0 · SOURCE_DEFECT · Owners: TASK-005, TASK-019, TASK-033, TASK-056, TASK-063.

Source observation: flushPending awaits executeDesktopAction but ignores the returned ok/outcome before mark_committed. A test on unsupported Linux expects a committed action; this proves bookkeeping, not successful desktop control. A rejected execution can also re-enter flushPending from finally while pending remains.

Required change: Separate dispatch acceptance, attempted side effect, verified completion and unknown outcome. Consume every result and caught exception. Quarantine an uncertain non-idempotent attempt until reconciliation. Retry only bounded classified safe failures; stop must prevent further dispatch.

Exit evidence: Adapters returning failed, unsupported, unknown, cancelled or throwing never produce verified success. A rejected or timed-out write cannot create an unbounded retry loop or duplicate note.

Anchors: src/main/metis-command-runtime.ts; src/main/metis-command-runtime.test.ts. [R80]

SRC-05 — Exact note, camera and close operations need real adapters

P0 · SOURCE_DEFECT · Owners: TASK-019, TASK-029, TASK-033, TASK-056, TASK-065.

30. Final architecture migration and execution checkpoints

30.1 One application, explicit responsibilities

The target diagram is a design contract, not a statement that all nodes already exist. Keep a thin React/Electron Windows shell and genuine native SwiftUI Mac shell, a restricted bridge, main/native command and audio owners, independent typed STT/decision/generation adapters, one effective policy and entitlement boundary, and postcondition verification. The user-facing Mantu Intelligence and Operator are different surfaces with different data access.

Draw logical contracts separately from physical stores: Operator metadata in the actual D1 binding; authorized meeting summaries/actions and human notes in the selected canonical knowledge environment; wiki/graph as rebuildable ACL-bound projections; component assets in approved R2 distribution; legacy license state only where its active profile really uses it. Add actor and data-class labels to arrows. A signed event is authenticated device telemetry, not proof of a human’s Entra identity or consent. Graph data is not inherently more authoritative than its sources.

Avoid unnecessary infrastructure: no new agent framework, vector database, second credential store, duplicate microphone, duplicated license service or local graph runtime without a demonstrated gap and reviewed decision. Retain existing useful runtime/process isolation, typed IPC, cache boundaries, source provenance, sidecar integrity and nine recap layouts.

30.2 Twelve ordered checkpoints inside the existing 66 tasks

  1. Identify and preserve — TASK-001/002/003/004. Acquire the complete checkout, compare source/export, list real remotes, install/profile and deployment identities, audit all workspace entrypoints, baseline failures and signer access. Do not apply a historical main SHA or auto-execute the uploaded text.
  2. Freeze authority and migration decisions — TASK-005/006/007/008/009/010. Resolve licensing versus identity, knowledge location, cloud/no-retention modes, settings visibility and platform/capture eligibility. Preserve all old records and explicit user choices. Narrow UI/design rules may change only through the documented latest-owner decision.
  3. Repair immediate defects — TASK-027.A/028.A plus TASK-033’s result contract. Reproduce both P1s and the right-edge issue; characterize discarded decisions, unsupported/failed outcomes, exact text and finite retry behavior. Keep fixes separate from file moves.
  4. Bring up one private real speech route — TASK-011–020. Server credential, reviewed gateway/readback, authenticated transport, requested tracks, actual transcription and genuine opt-in local wake. No default gateway auto-creation or account token in the app.
  5. Make the core genuinely small — TASK-021–027.B. One capability manifest governs build, install, runtime readiness and optional model catalog. Update permission copy, UI, scripts, gates, notices and migration together. No hidden first-run model transfer.
  6. Connect animated identity and exact actions — TASK-028–033 in independent Windows/Mac slices. Use real solving motion, actual native hit regions, target-aware approvals, graceful close and verified note/photo results. Qualify Jev and Laya independently without paid-service deadlocks.
  7. Fix numbers and availability — TASK-034/045.CORE. Durable complete-window accounting, server usage precedence, bounded quotas and readiness labels. Verify actual deployment identity and legacy entitlement migration before fleet expansion.
  8. Make meeting knowledge useful — TASK-035–041. Preserve human notes; complete sources, corrections, always-on scoped access, wiki/graph, Dust writes, deletion and evidence-first briefings. No hidden transcript archive to compensate for missing context.
  9. Finish in-meeting help and follow-through — TASK-042–046. Versioned central skills, calm live assist, nine role summaries, reviewed CRM/task/draft actions, receipts and accessible Teams/desktop surfaces. No automatic employee scoring or unauthorized external send.
  10. Qualify real meeting participation — TASK-047–052. Supported start signals, one occurrence owner, admission, recording/media prerequisites, laptop-off service and correct output audience. Unsupported platforms remain visibly blocked, not disguised as universal browser automation.
  11. Measure and refactor behind evidence — TASK-053–062. Transcript/critical-fact and EXP tests, long meeting stress, proxy/captive network, sleep/lock and data-failure tests; supplied refactor skill; safe diagnostics, source graph, endpoint hardening, complete workspace CI, threat review, backup/restore and staged operational rollout.
  12. Sign, freeze, qualify, promote and reconcile — TASK-063–066. Legitimate Windows signer before final tests; exact immutable artifacts, canonical update feed and real mirror authorities; verified post-upload download. Mac QA proceeds, while public Apple signing/eligibility remains separately gated. The final report includes every baseline, EXP and SRC disposition.

These checkpoints are not new root task IDs and do not impose a linear wait on independent providers/platforms. One integrator owns shared schemas, release metadata and migration. Product scope is not dropped just to satisfy a checkbox.

30.3 Often-missed enterprise behaviors

Network and long sessions: qualify authenticated proxies/PAC, TLS inspection under actual organizational policy, WebSocket disconnects, captive portals, sleep and credential renewal during a long call. Never disable TLS verification. Reauthentication cannot replay stale commands. A budget block stops new spending according to policy but preserves already approved local notes and truthful partial transcripts. Test service-region routes and rate limits with measured concurrency; historical vendor limits are not an SLA.

Workspace lifetime: sign-out, account switch, device lock and app quit invalidate capture/command generations, privacy-relevant caches and active approvals. Deactivation is distinct from deleting user data. A shared computer cannot show another user’s captions or restore their pending write. Crash recovery obeys the active retention policy instead of silently journaling raw transcripts.

Support and rollout: a content-free support bundle, bounded diagnostics, documented ownership and severity response, canary/rollback, source/dependency inventory and reproducible native evidence are release requirements. Scan every workspace and release artifact; a historical security audit, permissive fixture ignore rule or a healthy homepage is not current assurance. Use real load and usability tests, not synthetic dashboards as performance evidence.

End of lifecycle: authorized export, correction, revoke, uninstall, optional-pack removal, key retirement and disconnected service behavior must remain usable. Uninstall never silently deletes the selected external knowledge space. A stopped update feed or a pending Apple artifact does not get silently substituted with unsigned bytes.

30.4 Document and receipt drift prevention

Synchronize this canonical Markdown, generated PDF/HTML, task files, registry, visual state map and launch text. The older documents and mockups remain historical references only. A package verifier checks the 55 baseline requirements, 112 use cases, 66 root tasks, 44 commitments, 12 golden flows and 20 historical findings plus the 12 EXP contracts and 24 SRC findings. Every added item has existing-task ownership, explicit test definition and NOT_TESTED status until actual evidence is supplied.

A passing handoff validator proves structure, not semantics, deployment or best-in-class quality. A live benchmark must state sample, version, scope, environment, privacy basis, errors, abstentions and limits. New source/deployment/model/policy/ACL changes invalidate affected receipts. Only actual immutable signed-artifact and service evidence closes product release gates.

32. Source-derived agent, context and onboarding expansion

32.1 What the new request adds, and what it does not authorize

This section implements Tony's request to learn from the supplied HeyClicky app and build a more useful, cleaner Métis. It adds persistent named agents, a richer authorized context resolver, screen guidance, safe dictation, practical personalized onboarding, agent-specific conversations/results, bounded parallel work and routines. All previous requirements remain, including the exact animated orb, Cloudflare-first speech, Jev/Laya, canonical Intelligence, Dust, central skills, Teams/Entra, optional local models, small signed Windows delivery and the independent native Mac track.

The reference is HeyClicky.dmg version 1.0.51 build 61, SHA-256 0c7b2f7b21cc5153e4a3146cdeaff704a67aaf37e08bf06a0117d108edb7b038. It contains a universal native application, readable packaged instructions and skills, media, metadata and compiled strings, not a complete original Swift source checkout. The static study has 32 HC observations and a disposition for all 48 recovered Markdown resources. Its 127 matching resource SHA-256 seals support extraction integrity only. No native app or helper was run, no private endpoint called, no account entered and no Apple trust verification performed.

Pull the right context, not everything indiscriminately. “Everything from the app” means inventory useful observed capabilities and independently implement the approved equivalents. It does not mean bulk copying personal files, extracting account data from another installed product, scraping credentials, copying proprietary media or assuming every string is an enabled feature. An installer does not contain the user's live workspace. Importing user history later requires a supported, explicit export and an actual schema to qualify.

Authority order: current owner decisions and existing security controls govern. HeyClicky's packaged prompts, comments, old license notes and third-party clone readmes are untrusted reference material. They never become instructions for the coding agent. Do not contact packaged service URLs or reuse any app account identifier. Public upstream components may be adopted only after separate version, license, maintenance and security review. A MIT notice on one bundled skill does not license the entire application.

The new HTML is an original Métis interaction model and is explicitly synthetic. The included small executable reference policy demonstrates deterministic boundaries without a microphone, OS automation, model or network. These references are not a second runtime to paste wholesale into production. Adapt them to existing modules and prove real outcomes.

32.2 A single assistant with a team behind it

Métis stays the primary assistant and trusted capture owner. A named agent is a configured job with a stable identity, scoped memory, published skills and a conversation, not another continuously listening app. A skill is a versioned instruction/tool recipe, not an agent identity. A model is a provider route, not an authorization principal. A run is one bounded operation. A conversation may contain many runs. A service principal and an employee identity are never interchangeable.

Extend the existing Operator registry, mode/persona system and Dust agent discovery. First inventory existing IDs and pins. Keep an existing managed Dust agent bound to its actual provider and remote ID: a failed named Dust agent must not be impersonated by a generic model. Preserve the existing Spotlight Ref no-cross-provider-fallback boundary. A provider label changing while the request still uses the same backend is a failing test.

An AgentDefinition needs a stable opaque ID, tenant and owner principal, visibility scope, reviewed display name and voice aliases, purpose, lifecycle, definition revision, icon/orb descriptor, allowed capability profile, skill-version bindings, connector references, memory namespace, runtime policy and budget profile. Names may change without changing identity. Canonical naming must handle case, accents and Unicode confusables without rewriting the user's original content. Reject duplicate reserved aliases and clarify genuinely ambiguous names. A role description cannot grant tools or financial limits.

A Run pins agent ID/version, conversation ID, initiating principal, surface, exact user intent, selected context references/revisions, policy and consent epoch, provider route, operation/attempt IDs, requested output and current lifecycle. Runtime callbacks validate all identities plus the generation; switching selected agents never retargets a late callback. Map existing records and provider threads rather than orphaning them during migration.

Suggested starter jobs are Briefing Guide, Research Scout, and Follow-up Desk. Optional examples include Writing Studio, Workflow Guide and an explicitly approved developer assistant. These are proposed Métis names, not recovered HeyClicky defaults and not proof those services exist. Prefer the organization's actual published agents and skills, including authorized Dust agents. Users may rename a job without acquiring a new authority scope. Avoid a mandatory catalog of dozens of nearly identical personas.

32.3 Distinct agent orbs without a crowded desktop

Keep one neutral, animated solving orb as the only default ARMED desktop surface. No agent name, idle pill, caption, badge or sound is added to this state. The orb opens the existing main command surface; it does not spawn new microphone owners or always-on network sessions.

Inside the expanded surface, an explicit named agent uses a stable name bubble above the left edge of the bar, with the live caption above the bar and the orb inside it. Source chips sit in a separate row, not in the editable text or over Stop. Agent identity and task state are distinct: its identity motif persists while the current phase controls motion. A role must not use a success-colored orb as the sole proof of completion. The default Métis geometry remains the reviewed Libraries.dev solving animation; other agents may use a reviewed state-family/tint/initial combination from the same system. No arbitrary remote shader, script or user-uploaded executable avatar.

In the agent Home, each row has its name, one-line job, stable visual signature and understandable status. A busy-agent rail is opt-in, appears only for relevant active work and shows at most three named bubbles with a drawer for the rest. It must not permanently transform idle Métis into a parade of floating windows. Pending approvals take precedence over decorative activity. A background response never steals input focus, scroll position or the foreground app. A notification can open the exact owning agent/run on deliberate interaction.

Retain one window/geometry authority and per-display position. Bind pointer hit areas to visible content, especially while collapsed. Preserve the currently selected agent's draft, attachments, open result and scroll anchor independently. Keyboard navigation, meaningful accessible names, 200% text, light/dark/high-contrast and reduced motion must work. Names with long strings, emoji, accents and bidirectional text remain plain text, never HTML. Escape respects IME composition before application shortcuts; global Stop remains a separate trusted control.

Only visible active motifs animate. Pause when obscured, hidden, locked, reduced-motion or under the existing energy policy. A selected foreground orb may use the full motion budget; background tiles use throttled or static frames. Do not claim cost savings until measuring the entire process tree on real Windows and native Mac. The synthetic preview's frame count is not a power benchmark.

32.4 Context lens: know exactly what was read

Build a bounded ContextBroker over existing capture, preprocessing, file import, brain retrieval and connectors. Resolve the user's explicit target first. Prefer structured source access when available and authorized; use scoped native accessibility semantics for the foreground window; use screenshot/OCR or visual inference only when it contributes evidence the structured source cannot supply. A screenshot is not permission to operate that application's UI or read all of its account.

A ContextSnapshot records source kind, stable target/record ID, exact principal and tenant, selected app/window/document, relevant display coordinate system, source revision/content hash, capture/read time, extraction method, selected range, coverage (FULL, PARTIAL, VISIBLE_ONLY, UNAVAILABLE), excluded material, sensitivity, allowed audiences and revocable lease. Derived content keeps source references and cannot broaden access. Content never goes into the operational ledger.

Read the current document beyond the visible page only through a verified file handle, selected attachment, supported application API or authorized connector. Resolve user-visible identity and revision before claiming the read is for the active file. Unsaved buffers differ from on-disk files: show which representation was obtained; prefer an approved active-buffer API or label disk-only coverage. Do not silently read a stale saved file and describe it as the current unsaved document. For paginated remote sources, exhaust the selected authorized scope or disclose the exact gap; list counts are not full-text proof.

The user sees compact source chips such as Selected region, QBR brief · full text, Meeting summary · 09:00, or CRM · access needed. A details drawer explains method, age, coverage and exclusions. The chip can remove or replace context. Removing it revokes use in uncommitted work and invalidates dependent approvals; completed external effects require reconciliation, not retroactive claims that data was never sent.

Use bounded extraction limits, streaming parsers and input sanitization. Macro execution, embedded scripts and remote image loads are disabled in document previews. Do not run code from a workbook or document to extract its text. Exact text, spreadsheet values, slide notes, page references and layout-sensitive fields remain linked to the original. When a query needs an unread section, either acquire it legitimately or explain the gap. “I see everything” is forbidden UI copy.

Capture user context before opening the overlay; exclude all Métis surfaces from screen capture. Pin the original app/window so the assistant does not describe itself. Do not append all previous screenshots to every turn. Context refresh is demand-driven, relevant to the task and bound to policy. A previous screen grant is not consent to continuous organizational activity logging. Never read password fields, protected browser sessions or private folders simply to personalize a greeting. Permission acquisition belongs to trusted Settings/native UI, not an agent manipulating its own permission controls.

32.5 Read, teach, draft and act are different modes

Read returns evidence from selected sources. Teach points to a supported target and explains the next step. Draft prepares content without changing the target. Act performs an exact authorized operation and verifies its effect. Show the selected mode in the expanded interface; the model cannot silently promote a Read request into Act.

For screen guidance, use native overlay annotations anchored to a recent app/window snapshot. An annotation carries target identity, display, normalized region, age, confidence and expected role. Prefer accessibility element bounds; coordinate-only hints are explicitly weaker. Scroll, resize, zoom, display move, window replacement or stale target invalidates the highlight. Reobserve before pointing again. A circle or arrow is not a permission prompt and cannot click through to activate its target. Clear annotations on Stop, app switch where relevant, lock, tour exit or permission revocation. On Windows use real work-area coordinates; never emulate a Mac notch.

For dictation, pin the deliberate destination field before capture, maintain the original spoken text and separate a verbatim result from an optional rewrite. Do not remove negation, accents, product IDs, amounts or punctuation under the label “cleanup.” Before insertion, revalidate the exact application, field, selection and focus generation. If the target changed, preserve the text and ask for the destination or offer an explicit copy action. Never paste into a password field, a newly focused chat, a search bar or a terminal merely because it now has focus. When using the clipboard as a fallback, respect explicit choice and prevent restoring or overwriting a clipboard value the user changed meanwhile.

For actions, preserve API/connector-first execution when it can solve the task cleanly. Visible browser control is a separate route and normally uses a task-owned window or an explicit user-selected current tab. Do not clone browser cookies, profiles or login state, disable security preferences, run hidden debugging servers or use undocumented OS input injection merely to appear instant. Verify native API/support and deployment eligibility before adopting any upstream computer-use helper. Where only foreground interaction is supported, explain the handoff and pause when the user takes over instead of pretending invisible background control works everywhere.

32.6 Improve the existing Métis onboarding, never replace it

The owner explicitly requires Métis’s existing onboarding. Preserve the actual verified desktop order: Welcome (hero) → Problem story → Métis reveal → Appearance (“Where should Métis live?”) → Your setup → Personalization → [existing license branch only when enabled] → Ready. Preserve existing sign-in placement and completion/consent gates. The proposed r9 eight-stage wizard is superseded; it is not an alternative selectable onboarding. The native model’s existing topology must be reconciled explicitly, not replaced with a second coordinator. See §34 for the full source-bound contract.

Welcome: remove the old clip, poster, preload and remote fallback from active onboarding and final installers where exclusively used there. Add the authentic Tony Walteur recording in the same welcome slot with approved version/hash, rights, captions and descriptive transcript. Tony is the only named human in onboarding sample material. The film has not been provided: use the complete text-led Métis welcome, not a synthetic presenter, broken player or borrowed film. Video completion never supplies consent, permission, provider readiness or onboarding completion. Keep technical/legal attribution outside onboarding where required.

Problem and Reveal: retain Métis’s narrative and guarded real Bar/Copilot/Answer/QuickActions demonstration. Inside the existing Reveal teach typing as an alternative to voice, selected-context coverage and the distinction between a draft and sending. Example data cannot write real records or become service evidence. Preserve visible Next/Continue, original beat progression, teardown, reduced motion and safe keyboard/IME behavior.

Appearance: keep the placement choice immediately after Reveal. Seed the existing saved/managed choice; previews must not activate capture, auto-install a component or overwrite layout at finish. Keep native platform geometry, no Windows fake notch, and the existing single window/geometry owner.

Your setup: integrate account/policy readiness and progressive microphone, selected screen/system-audio, macOS Accessibility and relevant connector guidance into the existing rows. Explain benefit and access before a deliberate request. Opening System Settings means waiting, not ready; recheck the actual native grant and usable capability on return. Keep denied, restricted, restart-required, granted-but-unverified, unavailable and deferred states distinct. Windows uses its real capability flow. Do not auto-grant permissions or request all connectors to complete sign-in. Cloudflare is the speech default, with no organizational key on the device. Optional local model installation and selection remain explicit, device-qualified choices, not prerequisites for all onboarding.

Personalization: at most three optional goal/style questions within the existing mode/personalization area, with an editable summary. Do not infer personal history. Recommend up to three approved named agents here or in a post-onboarding sheet; show exact purpose/access/cost. Creating a definition is idempotent and starts no task, routine or paid execution. Existing pinned Dust identities remain intact. Optional connections and goals can be deferred without skipping the tour or marking them ready.

Ready and first value: the existing Ready completion remains the only finish after its actual consent rules. Separate onboarding completion, ready-to-type, actual speech readiness and first-value evidence. A real safe briefing/open-app/draft exercise can be offered within an existing practice area or immediately after finish; it is never a new compulsory scene or a fabricated demo success. Capability failure leaves truthful Retry, support and allowed alternatives, not a dead Continue button. No surprise capture occurs on finish.

Resume, replay and recovery: use the existing versioned progress owner, preserve layout, explicit local-only preferences, settings and existing agent IDs. Replay must not duplicate agents, grant permission, restart old media or run work. Bind async callbacks to principal, scene, request generation and revision. Pause media before permission handoff, actual microphone practice, scene exit, logout and application close. Consent is not derived from film progress. §34 and the synchronized onboarding preview are authoritative over old source comments and r9 replacement-wizard material.

32.7 Agent Home: useful work, not a settings maze

Home has a compact agent list, the selected conversation, relevant result preview and an optional context drawer. Keep the four everyday Settings destinations intact; agent creation is a task-oriented sheet or conversation, not another global settings category. One global action opens Home; one selection controls where a typed or push-to-talk request goes. The desktop orb remains quick access, not a second competing history store.

Messages preserve human originals, attachments, corrections and exact agent identity. Incremental/paged history uses stable record keys and bounded diffing; never rebuild hundreds of messages on every audio level, token or animation frame. Preserve scroll anchors when older turns load and when a reply arrives while the user is reading. Unread counts come from durable per-principal read markers, not the number of requests. Reconnect reconciles terminal states without duplicating turns or repeatedly surfacing an old failure as new.

Agent creation through conversation follows a bounded clarify → propose definition → confirm definition → persist → offer first task flow. Ask only necessary questions. “Make me three agents” can create three reviewed definitions atomically or with explicit partial results, but not three executing jobs. Bulk creation has a modest administrator-controlled limit. Archive pauses future routines and hides normal discovery; running work receives an explicit stop/finish choice. Delete follows canonical memory/artifact retention and rights rules, including shared records and legal holds. Rename changes labels only.

Display an inspectable job card: purpose, permitted data, allowed tools, connected accounts, memory scope, recurring schedule if any and recent verified results. This replaces an opaque personality prompt editor for ordinary users. Admins still manage actual policies centrally. A restricted or revoked agent may remain visible with a useful explanation, but cannot run from cached UI metadata.

32.8 Concurrency and control ownership

Different agents may research, draft or analyze independently through the existing bounded service scheduler. Do not implement each agent as a separate local Codex installation, speech recognizer, model process or unrestricted shell. Reuse one job coordinator with per-tenant/user budgets and per-agent isolation. The thin native client exposes only reviewed local actions under current policy.

Enforce one desktop input writer per enrolled device using a revocable lease with run/agent/principal/policy epoch and short heartbeat/expiry. A second agent waits or completes non-input work; it does not race for the pointer or paste into another agent's target. A read-only app snapshot still needs its own scope and must not be stolen from a different context. User input can suspend an agent's foreground operation. Lock, logout, permission revocation, Stop or lost authority prevents future dispatch; an already attempted action enters reconciliation, not an invented rollback.

Agent-to-agent handoff carries the user's original intent, minimal permitted context references and an explicit budget/authority intersection. A model-written handoff cannot expand the target audience or authorize new tools. Avoid delegation loops with depth/step/concurrency caps and cycle detection. A named agent cannot silently become another provider or another tenant. During execution, a follow-up correction queues under the exact run or starts a clearly separate turn; do not merge two conflicting instructions based on arrival timing alone.

Approval records bind the complete reviewed action list, target IDs, source/policy/agent revisions, side-effect class, destination audience, maximum cost and expiry. Source changes, agent switches, target replacement, altered output, additional steps or policy changes invalidate the approval. In particular, “do it” is not transferable across two agent bubbles or across a newly selected suggestion. Repeated approval cannot duplicate the write. Unknown outcomes block blind retry until the verifier determines whether the effect already occurred.

32.9 Routines and suggestions without noise or surprise spending

These are product capabilities to implement, not authorization to create any routine while drafting this plan. Require an explicit recurrence, timezone, target agent, permitted data/tools, delivery surface, budget, owner and expiry/review policy. Distinguish service work, which can run with the laptop asleep on an approved service, from device work, which waits for the specific enrolled unlocked device and required local permissions. Never migrate desktop control to a different computer as a “fallback.”

Every occurrence has an idempotency key. A missed run follows an explicit skip/coalesce/one-catch-up policy; do not execute a backlog of all missed intervals on wake. Offline, wait-for-user and waiting-for-budget are not the same as a failed paid attempt. Use backoff and a bounded consecutive-failure policy; pause recurring failures and show the cause. A pause or archive is checked immediately before dispatch and survives restarts. Expired user consent or provider quota cannot be bypassed by a cached schedule.

Proactive suggestions are read-only preparation under explicit source scope. Present at most a small useful set with sources, expected benefit, required permission and cost. One approval identifies one immutable suggestion; adjustment creates a new revision. Feedback is optional, scoped and deletable. Do not scan every connected app just to generate a morning greeting. Meetings, screen sharing, Focus/DND and quiet hours suppress unrequested speech, sounds and floating cards. Results remain discoverable in Home. No employee-productivity ranking or sentiment profiling is added to Operator.

32.10 Skills, connectors and rich outputs remain governed services

Map the reference's useful task families to the existing central platform: research briefs, email drafts, document/PDF/spreadsheet work, scoped knowledge retrieval, artifact lookup, approved application guidance and developer help where explicitly enabled. The file clicky-study/RESOURCE-DISPOSITIONS.json accounts for every recovered Markdown resource. A legacy guide for maps, personal messaging, location or financial-market data is not permission to enable it for every employee. Retain it as an explicit candidate until the owner approves a separately qualified relevant capability. Do not copy a disappeared UI feature merely because its asset remains in the DMG.

Provider and connector manifests report installed, configured, entitled, authenticated, scope-ready, tested and reachable separately. Runtime discovery must corroborate actual tool schemas. Handle pagination and selected fields instead of falling back to computer use whenever a large API response fails. Connect/reconnect belongs to trusted UI. Background agents cannot authorize their own OAuth, read login cookies, paste keys into chat or turn on broader browser automation permissions.

Each generated output gets a validated artifact manifest: owning agent/run, source revisions, type/MIME, exact storage handle, bytes/hash, creation and expiry, approved audience, rendering result and available actions. No absolute arbitrary local path supplied by a model. Preview content is sandboxed; external network/resources and executable content are blocked by default. Display files only after their actual bytes exist and any remote write is read back. Open, export, share and overwrite remain separate operations. Large outputs use bounded streaming and approved storage, not Cloudflare operational tables or logs. Unsaved/generated material cannot silently replace a user-authored document.

32.11 Portal and diagnostics prove the named-agent chain

Extend the single Operator ledger: employee, device, service identity, agent/version, skill/version, conversation, run, parent operation, provider attempt, phase, queue wait, actual duration and reported billing units. Product content remains outside telemetry. Display named agents and their bounded current state to authorized administrators without exposing private conversations, source titles or file paths by default. A heartbeat is not meaningful usage and a queued routine is not an active person.

Onboarding provisioning, agent creation, preview-only interactions, real task execution and routine occurrences have separate event classes. There is no free-standing “agent credit” counter that conflicts with token/speech/decision quantities. Report unknown usage as unknown. Two agents sharing one Jev key remain attributable and cannot see each other's inputs. A cancelled parent operation may still have one already-billed attempt; preserve that receipt honestly. Record exact deployment/policy/model versions for functional evidence.

Add a support view that answers “Why did Research Scout not finish?” using authorized metadata: permission blocked, source unavailable, device offline, stale target, queue waiting, quota, provider failure, verification unknown or published result not synced. A support export contains redacted operational identifiers and timings, not screenshot/transcript/prompt dumps. Content diagnostics are separate explicit governed workflows with their own retention, never enabled by a verbose flag in production.

32.12 Performance, independence and stronger quality gates

The reference bundle contains 589,486,538 bytes of CodexRuntime resources alone. Do not repeat that cost on every Métis device in the name of feature parity. Keep the existing cloud-first lean-core budgets. Agent catalog reads and activation must not install model weights. Shared hosted workers can carry approved execution dependencies; device helpers remain minimal and signed. Optional developer tooling is a separate administrator-controlled component, not the normal assistant's runtime.

Proposed UI budgets to qualify, not measured product claims: selected-agent switch without an uncached remote history read should visibly acknowledge within 100 ms at p95 on the agreed reference devices; local draft keystroke-to-paint p95 within 50 ms; a 1,000-turn chat must use pagination/windowing rather than render everything; a 20-agent catalog should not create 20 continuous animation loops; inactive animated surfaces perform zero frame work. Keep slow-network, cold-start and failure distributions. Onboarding targets first useful typed success within three minutes excluding unavoidable external consent; measure separately from real voice readiness. No skipped-task scoring trick or artificial delay for theatrical motion.

Test first-time and upgraded users, English/French/Portuguese/Spanish names and accents where supported, keyboard-only and screen-reader use, reduced motion, a muted computer, Bluetooth audio changes, permission denial, wrong tenant, expired connector, absent source, partial extraction, long history, background result arrival, simultaneous agents, user interruption, device lock, restart, migration and deletion. Scope and native-platform support are independent: a Linux browser demo is not proof of Windows automation, and Swift type compilation is not proof of a real Mac capture or action.

The strongest acceptance journey is one real enrolled user creating one named agent without starting it, selecting an authorized source, receiving a correct full/partial-coverage answer, explicitly approving a scoped side effect, observing a verified result, reviewing the same source-backed outcome through Mantu Intelligence/Dust, and finding accurate metadata in Operator. Repeat on Windows and native Mac where advertised; both Jev and Laya variants retain actual provider proof. Preserve the earlier meeting, transcription, privacy, licensing, native and signed-release gates.

No prior ID is removed or replaced. HC observations are artifact-derived evidence, not new product pass statuses. These 32 cases are separate from UC-001–UC-112 and remain NOT_TESTED until actual product qualification. Each applies on its advertised platform/profile; one browser reference does not close it.

AGUC-001 AGX-01 A reference contains an old unused skill picker. Classify the old note separately from live runtime proof; leave actual activation unverified.

AGUC-003 AGX-02 Ask about an off-screen clause in a selected document. Read the verified full revision through the permitted source or disclose visible-only coverage; cite the exact clause.

AGUC-006 AGX-03 The target moves, scrolls or changes displays. Invalidate the old highlight and reobserve; never point at the stale coordinate.
AGUC-007 AGX-04 Dictate Québec / R&D, do not send. Preserve accents and negation; insert only into the approved field without pressing Send.
AGUC-008 AGX-04 Switch to a terminal or another app before insertion. Block insertion; preserve the draft and offer explicit destination/copy choice.
AGUC-009 AGX-05 Create the recommended starter team then replay setup. Retain the same IDs and existing work; do not create duplicates or launch tasks.
AGUC-010 AGX-05 Video, speech or an optional connector is unavailable. Show truthful per-capability readiness, typed/static alternatives and working retry/exit paths.
AGUC-011 AGX-06 Rename Research Scout and change its orb. Preserve ID, provider pin, memory, jobs and permissions; no duplicate agent.

AGUC-013 AGX-07 Send a follow-up to a busy named Dust agent. Queue under its real remote identity and thread; no generic model impersonation.
AGUC-014 AGX-07 Say do it after switching to a different agent or suggestion. Require an exact pending target; never transfer the previous approval.
AGUC-015 AGX-08 Switch agents with unsent text and attachments. Restore each exact draft/attachment/scroll state without leaking it into another request.
AGUC-016 AGX-08 Delete or correct an agent fact and regenerate knowledge. Corrections propagate; deleted/revoked material cannot return from cached agent memory or old backup.
AGUC-017 AGX-09 Two agents request keyboard or pointer control simultaneously. One lease owner acts, the other waits; no mixed typing or cursor race.
AGUC-018 AGX-09 Cancel a run as its provider result arrives. Reject late dispatch, release authority, and reconcile any already attempted effect truthfully.
AGUC-019 AGX-10 A device-bound routine misses several runs while locked. Skip/coalesce per explicit policy and require that device to be eligible; no storm or device substitution.
AGUC-020 AGX-10 A suggestion finishes during a meeting or screen share. Keep it discoverable without unrequested speech, sound or sensitive floating text.
AGUC-021 AGX-11 Save a syntactically valid but rejected connector token. Show checking then rejected; never report Connected merely because it was saved.
AGUC-022 AGX-11 An API result is large or paginated. Use scoped pagination/fields; do not escape to broader GUI control as an automatic fallback.
AGUC-023 AGX-12 Publish a skill update while an agent is running. Current run stays pinned, later runs use the qualified revision, revoked versions cannot execute.
AGUC-024 AGX-12 A role requests a capability whose provider is not present. Report unsupported/unavailable accurately; do not loop on Retry or invent output.
AGUC-025 AGX-13 An agent generates a report and the user returns tomorrow. Find the exact durable result from its manifest without regenerating or changing ownership.
AGUC-026 AGX-13 A preview contains scripts, remote images or a traversal filename. Sandbox/reject unsafe content and paths; do not execute or exfiltrate.
AGUC-027 AGX-14 Two agents use the same server-held Jev key. Attribute both attempts correctly and keep user/context isolation; do not double count delivery retries.
AGUC-028 AGX-14 A preview or starter-agent creation is mistaken for a live task. Distinct event categories prevent fake usage/readiness; actual setup provider spend remains accounted.
AGUC-029 AGX-15 A catalog contains twenty agents and a thousand-turn chat. Do not create twenty frame loops or render whole history per keystroke; measure actual resource use.
AGUC-030 AGX-15 Reduced motion, high contrast, 200% text or IME is active. Keep names, source/approval controls and safe keyboard operation; no hidden required status.
AGUC-031 AGX-16 A new user completes agent creation, context, approval and result. Verify real source, backend, action, knowledge/Dust and Operator receipt on the exact candidate.
AGUC-032 AGX-16 The native or provider prerequisite is not available. Keep that slice BLOCKED and do independent work; do not pass it from HTML or another platform.

32.15 Ordered agent-expansion slices

Run ready slices in this dependency order alongside, not instead of, TASK-001–TASK-066. The urgent existing P1/input lane proceeds before optional agent work. Synthetic contract outputs allow independent implementation but do not enable customer content or public release. Native Mac and Windows runtime tests remain independent. The final agent claims feed TASK-055/056/057/060 and the actual advertised-scope gate in TASK-063 before TASK-064; high root numbers do not mean skip those acceptance conditions.

AGSTEP-01 — Reconcile the actual reference and source baseline

Root owners: TASK-001, TASK-002. Prerequisite slices: None. Gates: AGX-01.

Do: Identify the full current Métis checkout, all reference sources, rights, exact scope and existing agent/provider pins; retain early P1 lane.

Before closing: Actual source/contract/native/service proof at the level of the owned gates; satisfy root-task platform/provider prerequisites.

AGSTEP-02 — Freeze agent/context/authority contracts

Root owners: TASK-005. Prerequisite slices: AGSTEP-01. Gates: AGX-06, AGX-07, AGX-08, AGX-09.

Before closing: Actual source/contract/native/service proof at the level of the owned gates; satisfy root-task platform/provider prerequisites.

AGSTEP-03 — Build scoped context resolution

Root owners: TASK-033, TASK-037. Prerequisite slices: AGSTEP-02. Gates: AGX-02.
